{"etymology_number":"2","forms":[{"form":"forges","tags":["present","singular","third-person"]},{"form":"forging","tags":["participle","present"]},{"form":"forged","tags":["participle","past"]},{"form":"forged","tags":["past"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"verb","senses":[{"glosses":["To shape a metal by heating and hammering."],"topics":["arts","crafts","engineering","hobbies","lifestyle","metallurgy","metalworking","natural-sciences","physical-sciences"]},{"examples":[{"text":"The politician's recent actions are an effort to forge a relationship with undecided voters.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To form or create with concerted effort."],"synonyms":[{"word":"carve out"}]},{"examples":[{"text":"He had to forge his ex-wife's signature.  The jury learned the documents had been forged.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To create a forgery of; to make a counterfeit item of; to copy or imitate unlawfully."]},{"glosses":["To make falsely; to produce, as that which is untrue or not genuine; to fabricate."]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/fɔːd͡ʒ/","tags":["Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"/fɔɹd͡ʒ/","tags":["General-American"]},{"ipa":"/fo(ː)ɹd͡ʒ/"},{"ipa":"/foəd͡ʒ/"}],"upstream_line":264158,"upstream_offset":1319425756,"upstream_record_sha256":"9a1af4d7bbaf886771bebc012782af3bee1ccc781b32ab16da7154132969d796","word":"forge"}
{"etymology_number":"3","forms":[{"form":"forges","tags":["present","singular","third-person"]},{"form":"forging","tags":["participle","present"]},{"form":"forged","tags":["participle","past"]},{"form":"forged","tags":["past"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"verb","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"The party of explorers forged through the thick underbrush.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To move forward slowly (originally as a ship); to advance gradually but steadily, especially in the face of resistance or difficulty."],"tags":["intransitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"With seconds left in the race, the runner forged into first place.","type":"example"},{"text":"Let's forge past that runner on the inside.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To advance, move or act with an abrupt increase in speed or energy."],"tags":["intransitive"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/fɔːd͡ʒ/","tags":["Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"/fɔɹd͡ʒ/","tags":["General-American"]},{"ipa":"/fo(ː)ɹd͡ʒ/"},{"ipa":"/foəd͡ʒ/"}],"upstream_line":264159,"upstream_offset":1319448040,"upstream_record_sha256":"86c2767d3fe8638bb6ed36feda62c068c1e311e8a90fd752a6a5c45409d02a9e","word":"forge"}
